OUTLOOK                                                                                 展望
Despite the complex international and domestic economic and trade landscapes at         儘管當下國際國內經貿局勢錯綜複雜，中國經濟仍然展現
present, China’s economy has still demonstrated resilience, maintaining stable and      出了韌性，總體穩中有進。伴隨著國家擴內需政策舉措落
progressive growth. With the implementation of national policies and measures to        地見效，多個領域的消費市場已逐步回暖。我們也觀察到，
expand domestic demand, the consumer markets in various sectors have gradually          消費者的信心總體已趨於穩定，但各細分群體的消費意願
picked up. We have also observed that consumer confidence has generally                 參差不齊，消費者在支出時更加理性，也更加注重生活品
stabilized, yet consumption willingness varies across different segments.               質的提升。
Consumers tend to be more rational in spending while paying more focus on
enhancing their quality of life.

China’s apparel market continues to show diversified consumption trends, the            中國服裝市場繼續展現多樣化的消費趨勢，本土品牌在中
influence of local brands in the Chinese market and even on the world stage             國市場甚至世界舞台的影響力不斷增強，追求生活品味的
continues to increase, and the group of people who pursue distinguished lifestyles      人群持續增長，消費者對於個性化和可持續理念產品的需
continues to expand. The demand of customers for personalized and sustainable           求不斷上升，消費者對擁有強品牌力的產品或品牌好感度
products continues to rise and consumers’ preference for products or brands with        日漸增長，大家在追求物質消費的同時，也更加注重產品
strong brand awareness is increasing. People are drawn to the emotional value and       品質或服務所帶來的情緒價值和優質體驗，設計師品牌所
quality shopping experience brought by the quality of products or services, while       處的細分化市場潛力巨大。此外，消費者正快速從傳統零
pursuing material consumption. Therefore, the segmented market where designer           售向更多元化的新興消費場景轉移，數字化消費和電商平
brands operate has great potential. In addition, consumers are rapidly shifting from    台繼續發揮重要的作用，線上與線下渠道的融合加深，新
traditional retail model to more diversified emerging consumption scenarios where       零售模式進一步推廣。在此過程中消費者更加青睞他們更
digital consumption and e-commerce platforms continue to play an important role.        為信任的品牌以及可以帶給他們優質體驗的品牌，因此設
With the deeper integration of online and offline channels, the new retail model has    計師品牌所處的細分化市場呈現了向頭部集中的競爭趨勢。
been further promoted, during which consumers prefer brands that are more               這種趨勢也促使設計師品牌不斷提升產品質量和品牌形象，
trustworthy and brands that can provide them with a quality experience, therefore,      以滿足消費者對優質體驗的需求，同時也推動了行業內的
the segmented market where designer brands operate has shown a competitive              創新和升級。
trend of inclining to the leading brands. This trend also prompts designer brands to
continuously improve product quality and brand image to meet consumers’ demand
for quality experience, while also driving innovation and upgrading within the
industry.

As an influential designer brand fashion group in China as well as benefiting from      作為中國有影響力的設計師品牌時尚集團，受益於多元化
the diversified designer brand portfolio and sound operational management, we           的設計師品牌組合和良好的運營管理，我們對未來仍然充
remain confident about our future. Based on sufficient cash flow, we continue to        滿信心。我們將在保證充沛現金流的基礎上繼續鞏固及擴
strengthen and enhance our position as an influential designer brand fashion group      大我們作為中國有影響力的設計師品牌時尚集團的地位，
based in China, and we are committed to pursuing the following strategies thus to       並致力於創建一種我們所倡導的「江南布衣」生活方式生態
nurture the “JNBY” lifestyle ecosystem we promote:                                      圈而制定的各項策略，主要包括：

•     to continue to attract and cultivate new “JNBY” fans through further optimizing   •    通過自我孵化或併購的方式，進一步優化設計師品
      designer brand portfolio and product offerings by way of self-incubation or            牌及品類組合，繼續提升前瞻設計及研發能力，全
      mergers, through continuous enhancement of forward-looking design and                  面提升品牌力，繼續培養新的「江南布衣」粉絲；

      result in a breakdown throughout our network and our ability to continue               功開發、安裝、運用或實行業務發展所需的新軟件

      affect our results of operations. In addition, we may not always be                    需要較大資本開支，且我們未必能夠即時受惠於有
      successful in developing, installing, running or implementing new software             關投資。我們需要持續升級及改善我們的信息科技
      or advanced information technology systems as required by our business                 系統，以配合營運及業務的持續增長。
      development. Even if we are successful in this regard, significant capital

      operations and business.

NON-COMPETITION UNDERTAKING                                                               不競爭承諾
To safeguard our Group from any potential competition, each of Ms. Li Lin and             為保障本集團免受任何潛在競爭，李琳女士及吳健先生（「契
Mr. Wu Jian (the “Covenantors”) has entered into a deed of non-competition (the           諾承諾人」）各自已於二零一六年十月十三日以本公司為受
“Deed of Non-Competition”) in favour of our Company on October 13, 2016                   益方訂立不競爭契據（「不競爭契據」），據此，契諾承諾人
pursuant to which the Covenantors have unconditionally, irrevocably and jointly and       已無條件、不可撤回地以及共同及各別向本集團承諾，彼
severally undertaken with our Group that they shall not (except through the Group         等不會（透過本集團及透過本集團持有的任何投資或權益
and any investment or interests held through the Group), and shall procure that           則除外）並促使其緊密聯繫人（本集團任何成員公司除外）
his/her close associates (other than any member of our Group) shall not, during           於限制期間（定義見招股章程）不會直接或間接（包括通過
the Restricted Period (as defined in the Prospectus), directly or indirectly (including   代理人）以其個人或連同或代表任何人士、商號或公司（其
through nominees), either on his/her own account or in conjunction with or on             中包括）投資、參與、從事及╱或經營任何業務或於該等業
behalf of any person, firm or company, among other things, invest in, participate in,     務中擁有權益（在各種情況下，不論是否為股東、合夥人、
engage in and/or operate or be interested in (in each case whether as a                   代理、僱員或以其他身份），而該等業務與招股章程所述的
shareholder, partner, agent, employee or otherwise) any business which competes           本集團任何成員公司的現有業務直接或間接競爭或可能競
or is likely to compete, directly or indirectly, with the existing businesses of any      爭。

APPAREL MANUFACTURING AGREEMENT                                                     服裝生產協議

Ms. Li Lin (the “Founders”), thus, pursuant to Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules,    則第14A章，尚維服裝為本公司關連人士。
Shangwei Apparel is a connected person of the Company.

We entered into a framework apparel manufacturing agreement on December 25,         我們與尚維服裝於二零一五年十二月二十五日訂立及於二
2015 and amended on June 13, 2016 with Shangwei Apparel, pursuant to which          零一六年六月十三日修訂框架服裝生產協議，據此尚維服
Shangwei Apparel, together with its subsidiary, manufacture apparel for us. The     裝連同其附屬公司為我們生產服裝。服裝生產協議期限由
term of the apparel manufacturing agreement is from the Listing Date to June 30,    上市日期起至二零一九年六月三十日止。
2019.

Finery Co., Ltd. (“Shangwei Group”), pursuant to which Liancheng Huazhuo, and       議，據此，聯成華卓及尚維集團同意重續前框架服裝生產

agreement and Shangwei Group agreed to manufacture apparel products for us for      年七月一日起至二零二二年六月三十日止為期三年。上述

as set out in their respective terms of reference.

REMUNERATION OF DIRECTORS AND SENIOR                                                 董事及高級管理層的薪酬
MANAGEMENT

The Company has adopted a remuneration policy for the Directors and senior           本公司已採納一項董事及高級管理人員薪酬政策，總體原
management with the overall principle of motivating and promoting enthusiasm,        則為達公司戰略目標而激勵及推動各級員工工作熱情、積
initiative and spirit of the masters of employees at all levels to achieve the       極性及主人翁精神；
Company’s strategic objectives.

The purpose of the Company’s remuneration policy is to provide market competitive    公司薪酬政策目的是公司在合法合規的前提下，提供具有
compensation, attract and retain outstanding talents, and build a high-performance   市場競爭力的薪酬，吸引和保留優秀人才，組建高績效、
and high-quality team on the premise of legal compliance, so as to motivate          高素質團隊，從而激發員工效能與團隊生產力。
employee effectiveness and team productivity.

•     The remuneration strategy focuses on the principles of legal compliance,       •   薪酬策略著重合法合規性、市場競爭性、員工激勵
      market competitiveness, employee incentive, internal equity and cost               性、內部公平性和成本節約性原則；
      savings;

•     Offer market-leading remuneration for core functions and key positions that    •   對支持公司戰略實現的核心職能和關鍵崗位，提供
      support the Company to realise its strategies;                                     領先市場的薪酬水平；

•     The Company will continue to pay attention to the fairness of remuneration     •   公司持續關注薪酬給付的公平性，在滿足公司需求
      payment, and carry out reasonable remuneration cost control on the                 的前提下進行合理的薪酬成本控制；及
      premise of meeting the needs of the Company; and

•     For management purpose, the remuneration is adjusted annually based on         •   薪酬管理遵循以年度為調整頻率；以績效結果為薪
      performance following the process of reporting — review — verification —           酬調整依據；以報 — 審 — 核 — 准為薪酬調整流
      approval.                                                                          程。

Key Audit Matter   How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter
關鍵審計事項             我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項
                   We assessed the reasonableness of methods and
                   assumptions applied to the provisions by challenging
                   management’s future sales projection of quantities,
                   estimated future selling prices and selling expenses,
                   with reference to historical trends for the past
                   seasons on a season-by-season basis;
                   我們通過質疑管理層的未來銷量預測、預估未來售價及
                   銷售開支，並參考過去各季度的過季歷史走勢，評估撥
                   備所使用方法及假設的合理性；

                   We tested the ageing profile of inventories items on a
                   sample basis by checking to relevant documents,
                   including goods receipt note;
                   通過與相關單據（包括貨品收據）核對，我們在抽樣的基
                   礎上對存貨的庫齡結構進行了測試；

                   We tested the mathematical accuracy of the
                   calculation of the provision for inventories at the
                   balance sheet date;
                   我們於資產負債表日測試存貨撥備在計算時的準確性；

                   We observed the physical conditions of the Group’s
                   inventories during stocktake to identify if any
                   inventories were slow moving, damaged, or obsolete,
                   which were followed up in the management
                   assessment and the provision for such inventories
                   were included in the calculation of the total provision.
                   我們觀察 貴集團的存貨盤點期間的存貨盤點程序以識
                   別任何滯銷、受損或陳舊存貨，並在管理層評估中跟進
                   及將有關存貨的撥備納入總撥備的計算中。

                   Based on the above, we considered that
                   management’s assumptions adopted and applied in the
                   determination of impairment provision of inventories
                   were supportable by the evidence obtained and
                   procedures performed.
                   基於上文所述，我們認為，管理層在確定存貨減值撥備
                   時採用及運用的假設得到所獲證據及所執行程序的支持。

JNBY Design Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on                  江南布衣有限公司（「本公司」）於二零一二年十一月二十六
26 November 2012 as an exempted company with limited liability under the                       日 根 據 開 曼 群 島 法 例 第22章 公 司 法（一 九 六 一 年 第3號 法
Companies Act, Cap. 22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the Cayman              律，經綜合及修訂）在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限責任
Islands. The address of the Company’s registered office is Cricket Square, Hutchins            公司。本公司的註冊辦事處位於Cricket Square, Hutchins
Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman Islands. Pursuant to the                   Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman
resolution passed by the board of directors of the Company (the “Board”) on 8 June             Islands。根據本公司董事會（「董事會」）於二零一六年六月
2016, the Company changed its name from Croquis Investment Limited to the                      八日通過的決議案，本公司的名稱由Croquis Investment
present one.                                                                                   Limited更改為現時名稱。

The Company and its subsidiaries (collectively, the “Group”) are primarily engaged             本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主要於中華人民共和
in the design, marketing and sales of fashion apparel, accessory products and                  國（「中國」）及海外從事時尚服裝、配飾產品及家居用品的
household goods in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) and overseas.                    設計、營銷及銷售。

The Company completed its initial public offering and listed its shares on the Main            本公司於二零一六年十月三十一日完成其首次公開發售，
Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited on 31 October 2016.                           其股份在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。

These consolidated financial statements are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”), unless              除另有指明外，本綜合財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）列報。
otherwise stated.

3.1     Financial risk factors                                                          3.1     財務風險因素

The Group’s businesses expose it to a variety of financial risks: market risk           本集團的業務面臨多項財務風險：市場風險（包括外匯風
(including foreign exchange risk, cash flow and fair value interest rate risk and       險、現金流量及公允價值利率風險及價格風險）、信貸風險
price risk), credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management        及流動性風險。本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市
programme focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to             場的不可預測性，並尋求盡量降低對本集團財務表現可能
minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial performance. The Group      構成的不利影響。本集團目前並無使用任何衍生金融工具
currently does not use any derivative financial instruments to hedge certain risk       以對沖所承受的若干風險。
exposures.

(a)     Market risk                                                                     (a)     市場風險

(i)     Foreign exchange risk                                                           (i)     外匯風險

Foreign exchange risk arises when future commercial transactions or recognised          當未來商業交易或集團實體的已確認資產或負債以非該實
assets or liabilities of a Group entity are denominated in a currency that is not the   體的功能貨幣計值時，則產生外匯風險。本集團通過定期
entity’s functional currency. The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by            審閱本集團淨外匯風險來管理其外匯風險。
performing regular reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures.

The Group operates in the PRC with most of the Group’s transactions denominated         本集團在中國經營，本集團大部分交易以人民幣計值及結
and settled in RMB. The Group’s assets and liabilities, and transactions arising        算。由於本集團於二零二五年及二零二四年六月三十日的
from its operations do not expose the Group to material foreign exchange risk as        綜合資產負債表中大部分已確認資產及負債以本集團公司
the Group’s most recognised assets and liabilities in the consolidated balance sheet    各自功能貨幣計值，故本集團的資產及負債以及其經營活
as at 30 June 2025 and 2024 are denominated in the respective Group companies’          動中產生的交易不會使本集團面臨重大外匯風險。
functional currencies.

(ii)    Cash flow and fair value interest rate risk                                     (ii)    現金流量及公允價值利率風險

The Group’s income and operating cash flows are substantially independent of            本集團的收入及經營現金流量基本上獨立於市場利率的變
changes in market interest rates. The Group has no significant interest-bearing         動。除初始期限超過三個月的定期存款（附註21）、現金及
assets and liabilities, except for the term deposits with initial term over 3 months    現 金 等 價 物（附 註22）外，本 集 團 並 無 重 大 計 息 資 產 及 負
(Note 21) and cash and cash equivalents (Note 22). Those carried at floating rates      債。以浮動利率持有的資產及負債使本集團面臨現金流量
expose the Group to cash flow interest rate risk whereas those carried at fixed         利率風險，而以固定利率持有的資產及負債使本集團面臨
rates expose the Group to fair value interest rate risk.                                公允價值利率風險。

Loan carried at fixed rates expose the Group to fair value interest rate risk.          以固定利率計息的貸款使本集團面臨公允價值利率風險。
Management does not anticipate significant impact to the interest-bearing loan          管理層預期，公允價值利率風險不會對計息貸款產生重大
resulted from fair value interest rate risk, because the interest rates are not         影響，原因是利率預期不會出現重大變動。
expected to change significantly.

(iii)   Price risk                                                                      (iii)   價格風險

The Group’s exposure to price risk arises from investments held by the Group and        本集團面臨的價格風險來自於本集團所持有分類為按公允
classified as financial assets at fair value through profit or loss. To manage its      價值計入損益的金融資產的投資。本集團多元化其投資組
price risk arising from the investments, the Group diversifies its portfolio. The       合以管理因投資而產生的價格風險。本集團的投資旨在提
investments made by the Group are either for the purpose of improving investment        高投資收益或作戰略發展用途。
yield or for strategic purpose.

(b)    Credit risk                                                                         (b)    信貸風險

Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties default on their            信貸風險指本集團之交易對手未能履行其合約義務並對本
contractual obligations resulting in financial losses to the Group. Credit risk is         集團造成財務虧損之風險。信貸風險按組別基準管理。信
managed on a group basis. Credit risk arises from cash and cash equivalents, term          貸風險來自現金及現金等價物、初始期限超過三個月的定
deposits with initial term over 3 months, amounts due from related parties, trade          期存款、應收關聯方款項、應收賬款及其他應收款項。信
receivables and other receivables. The maximum exposure to credit risk is                  貸風險的最大敞口由綜合資產負債表中每項金融資產的賬
represented by the carrying amount of each financial asset in the consolidated             面值表示。
balance sheet.

(i)    Risk management                                                                     (i)    風險管理

For banks and financial institutions, only parties with good credit ratings are            就銀行及金融機構而言，僅具有良好信貸評級者獲接納。
accepted. For other receivables, the Group makes periodic collective assessment            就其他應收款項而言，本集團根據過往結算記錄及過往經
and individual assessment on the recoverability of other receivables based on              驗，對其他應收款項的可收回性作出定期的集體評估以及
historical settlement records and past experiences. In view of good cooperation in         個別評估。有鑒於與債務人良好合作的歷史以及良好的應
history with debtors and sound collection history of receivables, the credit risk of       收款項收款記錄，其他應收款項的信貸風險通常被認為很
other receivables is generally considered to be low. For distributors, the Group           低。就經銷商而言，經過考慮其財務狀況、過往經驗及其
assesses the credit quality of each distributor, taking into account its financial         他因素後，本集團將對每位經銷商的信貸質素作出評估。
position, past experience and other factors. Individual risk limits are set based on       個人風險限制根據管理層所定限制按內部或外部評級釐定。
internal or external ratings in accordance with limits set by management. The              信貸限制的使用受定期監察。所有該等主要經銷商通常均
utilisation of credit limits is regularly monitored. All of these major distributors are   具備良好的信貸紀錄。

Credit risk of trade receivables                                                           應收賬款的信貸風險

The Group applies the HKFRS 9 simplified approach to measuring expected credit             本集團採用香港財務報告準則第9號簡化方法計量預期信
losses which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables. To         貸虧損，該規定就所有應收賬款採用全期預期虧損撥備。
measure the expected credit losses, trade receivables have been grouped based on           為計量預期信貸虧損，應收賬款已根據共同信貸風險特徵
shared credit risk characteristics and the days past due.                                  及逾期天數分組。

The expected loss rates are based on the payment profiles of sales over lifetime           預期虧損率乃基於全期銷售付款情況及本期間內所經歷的
and the corresponding historical credit losses experienced within this period. The         相應過往信貸虧損。過往虧損率乃經調整以反映影響客戶
historical loss rates are adjusted to reflect current and forward-looking information      結清應收款項能力的宏觀經濟因素之現時及前瞻性資料。
on macroeconomic factors affecting the ability of the customers to settle the              本集團已將其銷售商品及服務之行業的國內生產總值及價
receivables. The Group has identified the GDP and price index of industries in             格指數識別為最相關因素，並根據該等因素的預期變動相
which it sells its goods and services to be the most relevant factors, and                 應調整過往虧損率。
accordingly adjusts the historical loss rates based on expected changes in these
factors.

As a part of this review, the Board consider the cost of capital and the risks          一環，董事會考慮資本成本及與已發行股本有關的風險。
associated with the issues share capital. The Group may adjust the amount of            本集團或會調整向股東支付的股息金額、向股東退資及發

4.     CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                                      4.     重大會計估計及判斷
       JUDGEMENTS

Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated and are based on historical                估計及判斷乃基於過往經驗及其他因素（包括在當時情況
experience and other factors, including expectations of future events that are                下對未來事件的合理預期）作持續評估。
believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.

The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the future. The resulting                本集團就未來作出多項估計及假設。按其定義，就此產生
accounting estimates will, by definition, seldom equal the related actual results.            的會計估計甚少相等於相關實際結果。很有可能導致下一
The estimates and assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material              個財政年度的資產及負債賬面值產生重大調整的主要風險
adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within the next                  的估計及假設於下文概述。
financial year are discussed below.

(a)    Net realisable value of inventories                                                    (a)    存貨之可變現淨值

Net realisable value of inventory is the estimated selling price in the ordinary              存貨之可變現淨值乃於日常業務中之估計售價，扣除估計
course of business, less estimated costs of completion and applicable variable                完成成本及適用可變銷售及推廣開支。
selling and marketing expenses.

The Group assesses the net realisable value of the inventories as well as the                 本集團評估每個資產負債表日之存貨可變現淨值以及所需
required amount of impairment of inventory provision at each balance sheet date,              計提的存貨減值撥備金額，此評估涉及基於考慮庫齡結構、
which involves significant judgement on determination of the estimated residual               未來銷售預測、預估未來售價以及銷售開支等關鍵因素就
value of the inventory based on the consideration of key factors such as ageing               釐定存貨估計剩餘價值作出的重大判斷。本集團亦對存貨
profile, future sales projection, estimated future selling prices and selling expenses        的毀損狀況進行定期檢查並於各資產負債表日就該等受損
of the respective inventories. The Group performs regular check on the physical               存貨評估是否需作出撇減。
conditions of inventories and assesses possible write-down for any damaged
inventories at each balance sheet date.

These key estimates are based on the current market condition and the historical        該等主要估計乃根據目前市況以及銷售類似類型產品的過
experience of selling products of similar type, which are reassessed at each            往經驗作出，並於每個資產負債表日進行重估，原因是其
balance sheet date as they could change significantly as a result of changes in         可能因為客戶喜好轉變及競爭對手因應嚴峻行業週期而採
customer taste and competitors actions in response to severe industry cycle.            取的行動而出現重大變動。

(b)   Right of return                                                                   (b)   退貨權

The Group offers right of return to distributors and end customers. The Group           本集團向經銷商及終端客戶提供退貨權。本集團管理層估
management estimates the amount of returns associated with sales in a specific          計與特定期間銷售相關的退貨金額，並從該等銷售產生的
period, which are deducted from the total revenue arising from such sales.              總收入中扣除。管理層基於歷史經驗使用預期價值法估計
Historical experience is used to estimate and provide for the returns, using the        退貨及就退貨作出撥備，且收益僅於極可能不會出現大量
expected value method, and revenue is only recognised to the extent that it is          退貨的情況下，方獲確認。管理層相信本集團已具備充足
highly probable that a significant reversal will not occur. Management believes that    過往經驗及模式，根據不同客戶情況，如經銷商、線下零
the Group has processed sufficient historical experience and patterns to estimate       售客戶、線上零售客戶等估計退貨。有關估計根據競爭格
sales returns based on different customer profiles, e.g. distributors, offline retail   局、經濟環境及客戶喜好轉變週期性進行。
customers, online retail customers, etc. Such estimates are performed on a
periodic basis taking into account the competitive landscape, economic
environment, and changes in customer taste.

(c)   Membership-based customer loyalty program                                         (c)   會員制客戶忠誠度計劃

The Group offers a membership-based customer loyalty program, under which               本集團提供會員制客戶忠誠度計劃，據此加入會員的客戶
customers who joined the membership are able to accumulate reward points                可通過購買商品積累獎勵積分並能將該等獎勵積分兌換為
through purchases of goods and could redeem these reward points for vouchers            後續購買中可享受折扣的抵用券。本集團基於預期兌換積
entitling discount on a subsequent purchase. The Group accrues for contract             分的估計獨立售價隨著會員積累積分產生合約負債。會員
liability as members accumulate points based on the estimated standalone selling        兌換獎勵時，應計合約負債相應減少。
price of the points expected to be redeemed. When members redeem awards, the
accrued contract liability is reduced correspondingly.

(d)   Determination of the lease term                                                   (d)   釐定租期

In determining the lease term, management considers all facts and circumstances         在釐定租期時，管理層考慮促使行使延期權或不行使終止
that create an economic incentive to exercise an extension option, or not exercise a    權之經濟誘因的所有事實及情況。僅在能合理地確定租賃
termination option. Extension options (or periods after termination options) are only   可延期（或不予終止）的情況下，延期權（或終止權後之期
included in the lease term if the lease is reasonably certain to be extended (or not    間）才會計入租期。
terminated).

       leased asset.

(e)    Accounting policy of revenue recognition                                          (e)   收入確認的會計政策

The Group is primarily engaged in the design, marketing and sales of fashion             本集團主要從事時尚服裝、配飾產品及家居用品的設計、
apparel, accessory products and household goods. Revenue from sales of goods is          營銷及銷售。銷售貨品的收入於產品的控制權轉讓予客戶
recognised at the point in time when control of the products is transferred to the       時確認。
customers.

In determining the transaction price for the sale of goods, the Group considers the      於釐定銷售貨品的交易價時，本集團考慮到可變代價的影
effect of variable considerations, and consideration payable to the customers. No        響及應付客戶的代價。概無重大財務部分被視為呈列為包
significant financing component is deemed present as the sales are made with a           含與市場慣例一致的信貸條款的銷售。
credit term consistent with market practice.

(i)   Sales of goods — distributors                                                      (i)   銷售貨品 — 經銷商

A significant portion of the Group’s products are sold to distributors, who have         本集團相當部分的產品售予經銷商，經銷商可酌情決定於
discretion over both the selling price and the distribution channels for such            其指定地理區域銷售該等產品之售價及經銷方式。經銷商
products to be sold in their designated geographical areas. Distributors are             一般須於作出購買訂單時支付按金，並須於產品交付前悉
generally required to pay deposits when placing purchase orders and are required         數結算。
to settle the full payment prior to delivery of the products.

Revenues are recognised upon delivery, which occurs when distributors pick up            收入於交付（即經銷商於本集團不動產提取貨品或貨品交
goods at the Group’s premises or when goods are handed over to a third party             付經銷商指定之第三方代理）時確認。交付乃於廢棄及虧損
forwarder as designated by a specific distributor. Delivery occurs when the risks of     風險已轉移至經銷商（即貨品根據銷售合約交付予經銷商
obsolescence and loss are transferred to the distributors, i.e., when the goods are      或第三方代理，或接收條款已失效，或本集團擁有客觀證
delivered to the distributors or the third party forwarders in accordance with the       據證明已滿足所有接收標準且概無可影響經銷商接受產品
sales contract, or the acceptance provisions have lapsed or the Group has objective      之未履行責任）時達成。
evidence that all criteria for acceptance have been satisfied and there is no
unfulfilled obligation that could affect the distributors’ acceptance of the products.

The Group offers volume rebates to distributors as agreed in the sales contracts.        本集團就銷售合約協定向經銷商提供批量返利。該等銷售
Revenue from these sales is recognised based on the price specified in the               的收入乃基於合約規定的價格，經扣除估計批量折扣後確
contract, net of the estimated volume discounts. Historical experience is used to        認。本公司利用歷史經驗採用預計估值法估計及提供折扣，
estimate and provide for the discounts, using the expected value method, and             且收入僅於重大撥回極大可能不會產生時確認。直至報告
revenue is only recognised to the extent that it is highly probable that a significant   期末，當預期向經銷商應付有關銷售的批量折扣時確認退
reversal will not occur. A refund liability (included in accruals and other current      款負債（計入應計費用及其他流動負債）。
liabilities) is recognised for expected volume discounts payable to distributors in
relation to sales made until the end of the reporting period.

(c)   The Group’s leasing activities                                                 (c)   本集團的租賃活動

The Group leases various offices and retail stores. Rental contracts are typically   本集團租賃多個辦公室及零售店。租賃合約通常有三個月
made for fixed periods of 3 months to 6 years but may have extension options as      至六年的固定期限，惟可能具有下文所述之延期權。租賃
described below. Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a     條款按個別基準磋商，包含各種不同條款及條件。租賃協
wide range of different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose     議並無施加任何契諾，惟出租人持有的租賃資產中的抵押
any covenants other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held   權益除外。租賃資產不得用作借款之抵押品。
by the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.

(d)   Variable lease payments                                                        (d)   可變租賃付款

Some leases contain variable payment terms that are linked to sales generated        部分租賃包含與商舖所得銷售額掛鈎的可變付款條款。使
from a store. Variable payment terms are used for certain reasons, including         用可變付款條款有若干原因，包括降低新開商舖的固定成
minimising the fixed costs base for newly established stores. Variable lease         本基礎。按銷售額計算的可變租賃付款於符合產生付款的
payments that depend on sales are recognised in profit or loss in the period in      條件之期間的損益內確認入賬。
which the condition that triggers those payments occurs.

(f)   Accounting policy of leases                                                       (f)   租賃會計政策

Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the      租賃乃在租賃資產可供本集團使用當日確認為使用權資產
date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.                       及相應負債。

Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group elects not         合約可能包含租賃及非租賃組成部分。本集團選擇將租賃
to separate lease and non-lease components and accounts for these as a single           及非租賃組成部分入賬為單一租賃組成部分，並無將兩者
lease component.                                                                        區分。

Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a present value   租賃產生之資產及負債初步以現值進行計量。租賃負債包
basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the following lease           括以下租賃付款之淨現值：
payments:

•     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments), less any lease            •     固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減任何應收租賃優惠
      incentives receivable

•     variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially measured   •     基於指數或利率之可變租賃付款，初步於開始日期
      using the index or rate as at the commencement date                                     以指數或利率進行計量

•     amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value guarantees       •     剩餘價值擔保下之本集團預期應付款項

•     the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is reasonably certain to     •     採購權之行使價（倘本集團合理地確定行使該選擇
      exercise that option, and                                                               權），及

•     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term reflects the   •     終止租賃所支付之罰款（倘租賃期反映本集團行使
      Group exercising that option.                                                           該選擇權）

Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are also           計量負債時亦包括根據合理確定延期權作出之租賃付款。
included in the measurement of the liability.

The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease. If     租賃付款採用租賃所隱含之利率予以貼現。倘無法釐定該
that rate cannot be readily determined, which is generally the case for leases in the   利率（本集團之租賃一般屬此類情況），則使用承租人之增
Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the         量借款利率，即個別承租人在類似經濟環境中以類似條款、
individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an          抵押及條件借入獲得類似使用權資產價值之資產所需資金
asset of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment      所必須支付之利率。
with similar terms, security and conditions.

(f)    Accounting policy of leases (Continued)                                            (f)   租賃會計政策（續）

To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                                   為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：

•      where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by the individual       •     在可能情況下，使用個別承租人最近獲得之第三方
       lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions          融資為出發點作出調整以反映自獲得第三方融資以
       since third party financing was received                                                 來融資條件之變動

•      uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free interest rate adjusted       •     使用累加法，首先就本集團所持有租賃之信貸風險
       for credit risk for leases held by the Group, which does not have recent third           （最近並無第三方融資）調整無風險利率，及
       party financing, and

•      makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term, country, currency and          •     針對租賃進行調整，例如期限、國家、貨幣及抵押。
       security.

Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance cost. The finance cost         租賃付款於本金及融資成本之間作出分配。融資成本在租
is charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant            賃期間於損益扣除，藉以令各期間的負債餘額的期間利率
periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability for each period.      一致。

Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the following:                        使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下各項：

•      the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability                           •     租賃負債之初步計量金額

•      any lease payments made at or before the commencement date less any lease          •     開始日期或之前所作之任何租賃付款，減去所得之
       incentives received                                                                      任何租賃優惠

•      any initial direct costs, and                                                      •     任何初始直接成本，及

•      restoration costs.                                                                 •     修復成本。

Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the shorter of the asset’s useful      使用權資產一般於資產可使用年期或租賃期（以較短者為
life and the lease term on a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain      準）按直線法計算折舊。倘本集團合理確定行使延期權，則
to exercise an extension option, the right-of-use asset is depreciated over the           使用權資產於相關資產之可使用年期內予以折舊。
underlying asset’s useful life.

A change in the consideration for the lease, without increasing or decreasing the         在不擴大或縮小租賃範圍的情況下，租賃代價的變動導致
scope of the lease, results in a remeasurement of the lease liability and a               對租賃負債的重新計量並對使用權資產作出相應調整。本
corresponding adjustment to the right-of-use asset. The Group remeasures the              集團使用租賃隱含的利率重新計量剩餘租期的租賃負債，
lease liability, using the interest rate implicit in the lease for the remainder of the   並對使用權資產作出相應調整。倘租賃隱含的利率無法可
lease term, and it makes a corresponding adjustment to the right-of-use asset. The        靠釐定，則本集團使用其修改生效日期的增量借款利率。
Group uses its incremental borrowing rate at the effective date of modification if
the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily determinable.

31. BUSINESS COMBINATION                                                            31. 業務合併
31.1 Acquisition of OōEli                                                           31.1 收購目里

(a)   Summary of acquisition                                                        (a)   收購摘要

On 28 November 2024, the Group acquired the entire interest in OōEli, a company     於二零二四年十一月二十八日，本集團收購目里（一間最終
ultimately controlled by the controlling shareholders of the Group. OōEli is        由本集團控股股東控制的公司）的全部權益。目里主要經營
principally engaged in the operation of brands named “B1OCK”, a boutique            名為「B1OCK」的品牌，B1OCK是一個融合當代藝術與生活
department store brand that blends contemporary art with lifestyle aesthetics.      美學的時尚精品百貨品牌。

The consideration is RMB1,672,000 in cash, which was determined between the         代價為現金人民幣1,672,000元，乃根據獨立估值師編製的
parties based on normal commercial terms with reference to the appraised value      估值報告，參考目里於二零二四年六月三十日的評估值，
of OōEli as at 30 June 2024, according to a valuation report prepared by an         經訂約方按正常商業條款釐定。代價已支付，及收購事項
independent valuer. The consideration was paid and the acquisition was completed    已於二零二四年十一月完成。
in November 2024.

31. BUSINESS COMBINATION (CONTINUED)                                                        31. 業務合併（續）
31.1 Acquisition of OōEli (Continued)                                                       31.1 收購目里（續）

(a)    Summary of acquisition (Continued)                                                   (a)   收購摘要（續）

The acquisition was considered as a business combination under common control               由於本集團及目里於收購事項前後均由同一控股股東最終
as the Group and OōEli are both ultimately controlled by the same controlling               控制且控制權並非暫時性，故收購事項被視為共同控制下
shareholders before and after the acquisition and the control is not transitory. The        的業務合併。收購目里乃根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港
acquisition of OōEli was accounted for using merger accounting in accordance with           會計指引第5號「共同控制合併之合併會計處理」
                                                                                                                 （「會計指引
Hong Kong Accounting Guideline 5 “Merger Accounting for Common Control                      第5號」）採用合併會計處理入賬。
Combinations” (“AG 5”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public
Accountants.

(b)    Merger accounting for common control combinations                                    (b)   共同控制合併之合併會計處理

Under merger accounting, based on the guidance set out in AG 5, the financial               在合併會計處理下，根據會計指引第5號所載之指引，財務
information incorporate the financial statements items of the combining entities or         資料包含發生共同控制合併時合併實體或業務之財務報表
businesses in which the common control combination occurs as if they had been               項目，猶如合併實體或業務自其首次受控制方控制當日起
combined from the date when the combining entities or businesses first came                 已經合併。
under the control of the controlling party.

The net assets of the combining entities or businesses are consolidated using the           合併實體或業務的淨資產按控制方的現有賬面值進行綜合
existing book values from the controlling party’s perspective. No amount is                 入賬。在控制方持續擁有人權益的條件下，共同控制合併
recognised in respect of goodwill or excess of acquirer’s interest in the net fair          時概不會就商譽或收購方於被收購方之可識別資產、負債
value of acquiree’s identifiable assets, liabilities and contingent liabilities over cost   及或然負債之公允價值淨額高出成本的部分確認任何金額。
at the time of common control combination, to the extent of the continuation of the         合併實體或業務之投入資本與相關投資成本抵銷之調整已
controlling party’s interest. The adjustments to eliminate contributed capital of the       於綜合權益變動表中計入合併儲備。
combining entities or businesses against the related investment costs have been
made to merger reserve in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.

The consolidated statement of comprehensive income, consolidated statement of               上一年度之綜合全面收益表、綜合權益變動表及綜合現金
changes in equity and consolidated statement of cash flows for the prior year have          流量表已予以重列，以納入所收購業務的業績，猶如收購
been restated to include the results of the acquired business as if the acquisition         事項自目里首次受共同控制當日起已經完成。於二零二三
had been completed since the date OōEli first came under the common control.                年七月一日及二零二四年六月三十日的綜合資產負債表已
The consolidated balance sheets as at 1 July 2023 and 30 June 2024 have been                予以重列，以調整所收購業務於二零二三年七月一日及二
restated to adjust the carrying amounts of the assets and liabilities of the acquired       零二四年六月三十日存續之資產及負債賬面值（財務影響
business which had been in existence as at 1 July 2023 and 30 June 2024 (see                見下文）。
below for the financial impacts).

31.3 Accounting policy of business combinations                                         31.3 業務合併的會計政策

The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                所有業務合併均採用收購會計法入賬，不論收購權益工具
combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are              或其他資產。收購一間附屬公司的轉讓代價包括：
acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary
comprises:

•     fair values of the assets transferred                                             •     所轉讓資產的公允價值

•     liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business                •     被收購業務前擁有人所產生的負債

•     equity interests issued by the Group                                              •     本集團發行的股權

•     fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent consideration    •     或然代價安排所產生任何資產或負債的公允價值，
      arrangement, and                                                                        及

•     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                 •     附屬公司任何先前存在股權的公允價值。

Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed in a    在業務合併中所收購可識別資產以及所承擔的負債及或然
business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair     負債（有少數例外情況），初步按彼等於收購日期的公允價
values at the acquisition date. The Group recognised any non-controlling interest in    值計量。本集團已以逐項收購為基準，按公允價值或按非
the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value or at   控股權益在被收購實體可識別資產淨值所佔的比例，確認
the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net         於被收購實體的任何非控股權益。
identifiable assets.

Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                     收購相關成本乃於產生時支銷。

The excess of the:                                                                      以下各項：

•     consideration transferred,                                                        •     所轉讓代價、

•      amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity, and                     •      於被收購實體的任何非控股權益金額，及

•      acquisition-date fair value of any previous equity interest in the acquired entity     •      任何先前於被收購實體的股權於收購日期的公允價
                                                                                                     值

over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is recorded as goodwill. If       超過所收購可識別資產淨值公允價值的差額，乃入賬列作
those amounts are less than the fair value of the net identifiable assets of the              商譽。若該等金額低於所收購業務的可識別資產淨值的公
business acquired, the difference is recognised directly in profit or loss as a               允價值，則該差額會直接於損益內確認為議價收購。
bargain purchase.

Where settlement of any part of cash consideration is deferred, the amounts                   倘遞延結算任何部分現金代價，則日後應付款項貼現至彼
payable in the future are discounted to their present value as at the date of                 等於兌換日期的現值。所用的貼現率乃該實體的增量借款
exchange. The discount rate used is the entity’s incremental borrowing rate, being            利率，即根據可資比較條款及條件可從獨立金融機構獲得
the rate at which a similar borrowing could be obtained from an independent                   類似借款的利率。或然代價分類為權益或金融負債。分類
financial institution under comparable terms and conditions. Contingent                       為金融負債的金額隨後重新計量至其公允價值，而公允價
consideration is classified either as equity or a financial liability. Amounts classified     值變動則於損益內確認。
as a financial liability are subsequently remeasured to fair value with changes in
fair value recognised in profit or loss.

If the business combination is achieved in stages, the carrying value of the                  倘業務合併分階段進行，收購方先前持有的被收購方股權
acquirer’s previously held equity interest in the acquiree at the acquisition date is         於收購日期的賬面值按收購日期的公允價值重新計量。重
remeasured to fair value at the acquisition date. Any gains or losses arising from            新計量產生的任何收益或虧損於損益確認。
such remeasurement are recognised in profit or loss.

36. SUMMARY OF OTHER ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                36. 其他會計政策概要
36.1 Foreign currency translation                                                       36.1 外幣折算

(a)   Functional and presentation currency                                              (a)   功能及列報貨幣

Items included in the financial statements of each of the Group’s entities are          本集團各實體財務報表所列的項目均以該實體經營所在主
measured using the currency of the primary economic environment in which the            要 經 濟 環 境 所 使 用 的 貨 幣（「功 能 貨 幣」）計 量。本 公 司、
entity operates (the “functional currency”). The functional currency of the Company,    Croquis Holdings Limited及廣益（中國）有限公司的功能貨
Croquis Holdings Limited and Grand Vantage (China) Limited is the HK$. The              幣為港元。於中國內地註冊成立的附屬公司視人民幣為彼

which there are separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units). Non-         否轉回進行檢討。

36.5.4 Impairment                                                                          36.5.4 減值

The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the expected credit losses                   本集團按前瞻性原則，對按攤銷成本及按公允價值計入全
associated with its debt instruments carried at amortised cost and FVOCI. The              面收益列賬的債務工具相關的預期信貸虧損進行評估。所
impairment methodology applied depends on whether there has been a significant             採用的減值方法取決於信貸風險是否大幅增加。
increase in credit risk.

For trade receivables, the Group applies the simplified approach permitted by              就應收賬款而言，本集團採用香港財務報告準則第9號允許
HKFRS 9, which requires expected lifetime losses to be recognised from initial             的簡化方法，其中要求全期預期虧損須自初始確認應收款
recognition of the receivables, see Note 17 for further details. The Group has             項時確認，有關更多詳情請參閱附註17。本集團已根據其
established a provision matrix that is based on its historical credit loss experience,     歷史信貸虧損訂立撥備矩陣，且就債務人及經濟環境的特
adjusted for forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic              定前瞻性因素作出調整。
environment.

For deposits and other receivables, management considers that its credit risk has          就按金及其他應收款項而言，管理層認為，經參考對手方
not increased significantly since initial recognition with reference to the                歷史違約率及當前財務狀況後，其信貸風險自初始確認後
counterparty historical default rate and current financial position. The impairment        並無大幅增加。按12個月預期信貸虧損釐定的減值撥備接
provision is determined based on the 12-month expected credit losses, which is             近零。為評估信貸風險是否大幅增加，本集團對比資產於
close to zero. To assess whether there is a significant increase in credit risk, the       報告日期的違約風險，違約風險透過計入可得、合理及可
Group compares the risk of default occurring on the asset as at the reporting date         靠的前瞻性資料於初步確認日期計量。
with the risk of default as at the date of initial recognition by considering available,
reasonable and supportive forwarding-looking information.

36.6 Offsetting financial instruments                                                      36.6 抵銷金融工具

Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount reported in the             當有法定可強制執行權利可抵銷已確認金額，並有意按淨
balance sheet when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised           額基準結算或同時變現資產和結算負債時，金融資產與負
amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and        債可互相抵銷，並在資產負債表呈報其淨額。法定可強制
settle the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not be             執行權利不得依賴未來事件而定，且在一般業務過程中以
contingent on future events and must be enforceable in the normal course of                及倘公司或對手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或破產時，
business and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the company or           須具有可強制執行性。
the counterparty.

36.7 Trade and other receivables                                                           36.7 應收賬款及其他應收款項

Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for merchandise sold in the               應收賬款為在日常業務過程中就售出貨品應收客戶的款項。
ordinary course of business. If collection of trade and other receivables is expected      如應收賬款及其他應收款項預期在一年或以內（如在業務
in one year or less (or in the normal operating cycle of the business if longer), they     的正常營運週期，則可較長時間）收回，分類為流動資產，
are classified as current assets. If not, they are presented as non-current assets.        否則分類為非流動資產。

Trade and other receivables are recognised initially at fair value and subsequently        應收賬款及其他應收款項按公允價值初始確認，其後以實
measured at amortised cost using the effective interest method, less provision for         際利率法按攤銷成本扣除減值撥備計量。有關本集團減值
impairment. See Note 36.4 for description of the Group’s impairment policies.              政策的闡述請參閱附註36.4。

36.9 Borrowings                                                                             36.9 借款

Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of transaction costs incurred.       借款按公允價值（扣除所產生的交易成本）初始確認。借款
Borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost. Any difference between              其後按攤銷成本計量。所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖回款
the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption amount is recognised in          項之間的任何差額以實際利率法於借款期間內於損益確認。
profit or loss over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.       倘貸款融資很有可能部分或全部被提取，則就設立貸款融
Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as transaction             資時支付的費用確認為貸款交易成本。在此情況下，費用
costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility will   遞延至提取發生為止。倘無任何證據顯示該貸款很有可能
be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the         部分或全部提取，則該費用撥充資本作為流動資金服務的
extent there is no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be    預付款，並於有關融資期間攤銷。
drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity services and
amortised over the period of the facility to which it relates.

Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless, at the end of the reporting        除非本集團於報告期末有權將負債結算日期押後至報告期
period, the Group has a right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12          間後至少12個月，否則借款將被分類為流動負債。
months after the reporting period.

36.10 Current and deferred income tax                                                       36.10 當期及遞延所得稅

The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on the current           期內所得稅開支或抵免指根據各司法管轄權區的適用所得
period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for each                    稅率按當期應課稅收入支付的稅項，而有關所得稅率經暫
jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to     時性差額及未動用稅項虧損所致的遞延稅項資產及負債變
temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                                             動調整。

(a)    Current income tax                                                                   (a)   當期所得稅

The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax laws enacted or         當期所得稅開支乃根據本公司的附屬公司及聯營公司營運
substantively enacted at the balance sheet date in the countries where the                  及產生應課稅收入所在國家按於資產負債表日已頒佈或實
Company’s subsidiaries and associates operate and generate taxable income.                  質上已頒佈的稅法計算。管理層就適用稅務法例詮釋所規
Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to            限的情況定期評估報稅表的狀況，並在適用情況下按預期
situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation. It              須向稅務機關支付的稅款作出撥備。
establishes provisions where appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be
paid to the tax authorities.

Inside basis differences                                                                 內在差異

carrying amounts in the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred tax         遞延稅項負債來自對商譽的初步確認，則不予確認；以及

Deferred income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been              稅負債結算時將應用的稅率（及法例）釐定。

amounts will be available to utilise those temporary differences and losses.             暫時性差額及虧損時確認。

Outside basis differences                                                                外在差異

Deferred income tax liabilities are provided on taxable temporary differences            遞延所得稅負債按於附屬公司的投資所產生的應課稅暫時
arising from investments in subsidiaries, except for deferred income tax liability       性差額作撥備，惟倘就遞延所得稅負債而言，撥回暫時性
where the timing of the reversal of the temporary difference is controlled by the        差額的時間由本集團控制，而在可見將來不大可能撥回暫
Group and it is probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in the           時性差額則除外。
foreseeable future.

Deferred income tax assets are recognised on deductible temporary differences            遞延所得稅資產僅會於暫時性差額有可能於未來撥回及有
arising from investments in subsidiaries, only to the extent that it is probable the     充足應課稅利潤可用以動用暫時性差額時就附屬公司投資
temporary difference will reverse in the future and there is sufficient taxable profit   產生的可扣稅暫時性差額確認。
available against which the temporary difference can be utilised.

(c)   Offsetting                                                                         (c)   抵銷

right to offset current tax assets and liabilities and where the deferred tax            遞延稅項結餘與同一稅務機構相關時，則可將遞延稅項資
balances relate to the same taxation authority. Current tax assets and tax liabilities   產與負債抵銷。當實體有法定可強制執行權利抵銷且有意
are offset where the entity has a legally enforceable right to offset and intends        按淨額基準結算或同時變現資產及清償負債時，則當期稅
either to settle on a net basis, or to realise the asset and settle the liability        項資產與稅項負債互相抵銷。
simultaneously.
